<!-- ty-source-item:start key=requirement-selected-current-resource-operations kind=requirement -->
The current Sky Canvas resource is the explicitly selected implementation constraint for operations; DESIGN.md remains the exact token owner and production Source remains the data, permission and business-rule owner.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-development-consumption-boundary-operations kind=control -->
Development must consume the complete selected Source closure and structured handoff, map fixture states to the existing production stores and ports, preserve truthful loading/partial/stale/error/permission states, and independently verify the real route.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-01 kind=control -->
family.operations-shell: Queue, case and governance work share one authenticated shell without implying server authority. Data binding: authenticated operator identity and API status. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: loading, ready, saving, error.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-02 kind=control -->
family.moderation-queue: Priority, age, kind, location and risk remain scannable; opening a row preserves queue context. Data binding: moderation queue projection. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: loading, ready, empty, filtered, error.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-03 kind=control -->
family.moderation-case: Decision reason and history are separate from immutable original evidence. Data binding: submission evidence and review decision. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: reviewing, changes-requested, accepted, rejected, withdrawn.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-04 kind=control -->
family.media-review: Raw path, EXIF and precise contributor coordinates are absent from ordinary reads. Data binding: sanitized derivatives and media provenance. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: loading, ready, unsafe, unavailable.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-05 kind=control -->
family.canonical-merge: Preview and commit are separate audited steps; retry remains idempotent. Data binding: canonical evidence merge command. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: not-started, preview, ready, committing, merged, superseded, failed.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-06 kind=control -->
family.publication-assessment: Publish remains unavailable while any required evidence is missing or invalid. Data binding: server-owned formal-spot completeness assessment. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: checking, blocked, eligible, publishing, published, failed.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-07 kind=control -->
family.replacement-retirement: Every transition requires reason, actor, result and readback. Data binding: formal spot publication lifecycle. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: active, suspended, unpublished, replaced, retired, failed.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-08 kind=control -->
family.audit-timeline: Actor, action, object, reason, timestamp and result remain attributable. Data binding: immutable audit projection. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: loading, ready, empty, filtered, error.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-09 kind=control -->
family.operations-status-recovery: A client success state never substitutes for server receipt and readback. Data binding: operations request lifecycle. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/page.tsx. States: loading, ready, empty, partial, stale, error.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->

<!-- ty-source-item:start key=control-operations-10 kind=control -->
family.operations-semantic-icon: Icons reinforce labels and never carry the only action or state meaning. Data binding: semantic icon role. Production owner candidate: apps/admin-web/src/app/styles.css. States: default, active, disabled.
<!-- ty-source-item:end -->
